\documentclass[10pt]{article}

\begin{document}
\begin{center}\Large SRI (k-inflation): N04LO\end{center}
The spectra are evaluated at $-k_*\eta_*=1$, through relative amplitude
order $p=4$. The Hubble-flow parameters are denoted by $\epsilon_j$
and $M_{\rm Pl}$ is the reduced Planck mass. Coefficients are generated
numerically from the normalized mode equations, using the iterative algebra.
The convention for the spectral derivatives is
\[
\alpha_n^A=\frac{d^{n+1}\ln P_A}{d(\ln k)^{n+1}},\qquad
\alpha_0^s=n_s-1,\qquad\alpha_0^t=n_t,\qquad r=P_t/P_s.
\]
For k-inflation, $c_t=1$. The scalar amplitude includes transport from the acoustic reference to the common tensor reference. The sound speed is $c_{s*}>0$, with
\[\alpha_n^{c_s}=\frac{d^{n+1}\ln c_s}{d(\ln k)^{n+1}}.\]
\section{Spectra}

\subsection{Scalar spectrum}
\[P_s(k_*)=\frac{H_*^2}{8\pi^2M_{\rm Pl}^2\epsilon_{1*}c_{s*}}\left[\sum_{d=0}^{4}F_{s,d}+O(\mathrm{SR}^{5})\right].\]
\paragraph{Relative order 0.}
\begin{align*}
F_{s,0} &= 1.0
\end{align*}

\paragraph{Relative order 1.}
\begin{align*}
F_{s,1} &= 0.72963715453852 \epsilon_{2} - 0.54072569092296 \epsilon_{1} \\
&{}  - 1.2703628454615 \alpha_{0}^{c_s} - 1.0 \alpha_{0}^{c_s} \log{\left(c_{s} \right)} \\
&{}  - 1.0 \epsilon_{2} \log{\left(c_{s} \right)} - 2.0 \epsilon_{1} \log{\left(c_{s} \right)}
\end{align*}

\paragraph{Relative order 2.}
\begin{align*}
F_{s,2} &= 1.5402686460338 \epsilon_{1}^{2} + 0.49988573877771 \epsilon_{2}^{2} \\
&{}  - 0.39567736285244 \alpha_{1}^{c_s} - 0.22975141576082 \left(\alpha_{0}^{c_s}\right)^{2} \\
&{}  + 0.5 \left(\alpha_{0}^{c_s}\right)^{2} \log{\left(c_{s} \right)}^{2} + 0.5 \epsilon_{2}^{2} \log{\left(c_{s} \right)}^{2} \\
&{}  + 2.0 \epsilon_{1}^{2} \log{\left(c_{s} \right)}^{2} + 0.27036284546148 \left(\alpha_{0}^{c_s}\right)^{2} \log{\left(c_{s} \right)} \\
&{}  + 1.0192767657904 \epsilon_{1} \epsilon_{2} + 1.0809943369567 \alpha_{0}^{c_s} \epsilon_{1} \\
&{}  + 0.14504832807052 \epsilon_{2} \epsilon_{3} - 0.5 \alpha_{1}^{c_s} \log{\left(c_{s} \right)}^{2} \\
&{}  - 0.91854861815409 \epsilon_{1}^{2} \log{\left(c_{s} \right)} - 0.72986567698311 \alpha_{0}^{c_s} \epsilon_{2} \\
&{}  - 1.2703628454615 \alpha_{1}^{c_s} \log{\left(c_{s} \right)} - 0.72963715453852 \epsilon_{2}^{2} \log{\left(c_{s} \right)} \\
&{}  + 1.0 \alpha_{0}^{c_s} \epsilon_{2} \log{\left(c_{s} \right)}^{2} + 1.0 \epsilon_{1} \epsilon_{2} \log{\left(c_{s} \right)}^{2} \\
&{}  + 2.0 \alpha_{0}^{c_s} \epsilon_{1} \log{\left(c_{s} \right)}^{2} + 1.0814513818459 \alpha_{0}^{c_s} \epsilon_{1} \log{\left(c_{s} \right)} \\
&{}  + 0.72963715453852 \epsilon_{2} \epsilon_{3} \log{\left(c_{s} \right)} - 0.5 \epsilon_{2} \epsilon_{3} \log{\left(c_{s} \right)}^{2} \\
&{}  - 0.45927430907704 \alpha_{0}^{c_s} \epsilon_{2} \log{\left(c_{s} \right)} \\
&{}  - 2.459274309077 \epsilon_{1} \epsilon_{2} \log{\left(c_{s} \right)}
\end{align*}

\paragraph{Relative order 3.}

\begin{align*}

\subsection{Tensor spectrum}
\[P_t(k_*)=\frac{2H_*^2}{\pi^2M_{\rm Pl}^2}\left[\sum_{d=0}^{4}F_{t,d}+O(\mathrm{SR}^{5})\right].\]
\paragraph{Relative order 0.}
\begin{align*}
F_{t,0} &= 1.0
\end{align*}

\paragraph{Relative order 1.}
\begin{align*}
F_{t,1} &=  - 0.54072569092296 \epsilon_{1}
\end{align*}

\paragraph{Relative order 2.}
\begin{align*}
F_{t,2} &= 1.5402686460338 \epsilon_{1}^{2} - 0.25062903478191 \epsilon_{1} \epsilon_{2}
\end{align*}

\paragraph{Relative order 3.}
\begin{align*}
F_{t,3} &= 1.6057835635673 \epsilon_{1}^{3} + 1.8891060194179 \epsilon_{1}^{2} \epsilon_{2} \\
&{}  - 0.18929040711048 \epsilon_{1} \epsilon_{2}^{2} - 0.18929040711048 \epsilon_{1} \epsilon_{2} \epsilon_{3}
\end{align*}

\paragraph{Relative order 4.}
\begin{align*}
F_{t,4} &= 2.9412556561294 \epsilon_{1}^{4} + 4.0859368676311 \epsilon_{1}^{3} \epsilon_{2} \\
&{}  + 1.8087367742501 \epsilon_{1}^{2} \epsilon_{2}^{2} - 0.077715927178017 \epsilon_{1} \epsilon_{2}^{3} \\
&{}  + 1.696905383796 \epsilon_{1}^{2} \epsilon_{2} \epsilon_{3} - 0.077715927178017 \epsilon_{1} \epsilon_{2} \epsilon_{3}^{2} \\
&{}  - 0.23314778153405 \epsilon_{1} \epsilon_{2}^{2} \epsilon_{3} - 0.077715927178017 \epsilon_{1} \epsilon_{2} \epsilon_{3} \epsilon_{4}
\end{align*}

\section{Consistency relations}
\subsection{$n=0$}
For $p=4$, the absolute cutoff is $W=5$:
\[\alpha_{0}^t=\sum_{d=1}^{5}R_{0,d}+O(\mathrm{SR}^{6}).\]
\paragraph{Absolute order 1.}
\begin{align*}
R_{0,1} &=  - \frac{0.125 r}{c_{s}}
\end{align*}

\paragraph{Absolute order 2.}
\begin{align*}
R_{0,2} &= \frac{0.0078125 r^{2}}{c_{s}^{2}} + \frac{0.125 \alpha_{0}^s r}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  + \frac{0.28379535568268 \alpha_{0}^{c_s} r}{c_{s}} - \frac{0.125 \alpha_{0}^s r \log{\left(c_{s} \right)}}{c_{s}}
\end{align*}

\paragraph{Absolute order 3.}
\begin{align*}
R_{0,3} &=  - \frac{0.0030712246746565 r^{3}}{c_{s}^{3}} + \frac{0.125 \alpha_{1}^s r}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  + \frac{0.33325502603924 \alpha_{1}^{c_s} r}{c_{s}} - \frac{0.10280837917801 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{2} r}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  - \frac{0.20825502603924 \left(\alpha_{0}^{c_s}\right)^{2} r}{c_{s}} - \frac{0.041327094794504 \alpha_{0}^s r^{2}}{c_{s}^{2}} \\
&{}  - \frac{0.061176514254839 \alpha_{0}^{c_s} r^{2}}{c_{s}^{2}} + \frac{0.015625 \alpha_{0}^s r^{2} \log{\left(c_{s} \right)}}{c_{s}^{2}} \\
&{}  + \frac{0.0625 \alpha_{1}^s r \log{\left(c_{s} \right)}^{2}}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  + \frac{0.125 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{2} r \log{\left(c_{s} \right)}}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  - \frac{0.0625 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{2} r \log{\left(c_{s} \right)}^{2}}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  - \frac{0.125 \alpha_{1}^s r \log{\left(c_{s} \right)}}{c_{s}} - \frac{0.46478233218736 \alpha_{0}^{c_s} \alpha_{0}^s r}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  + \frac{0.28379535568268 \alpha_{0}^{c_s} \alpha_{0}^s r \log{\left(c_{s} \right)}}{c_{s}}
\end{align*}

\paragraph{Absolute order 4.}
\begin{align*}
R_{0,4} &= \frac{0.0014926374043055 r^{4}}{c_{s}^{4}} + \frac{0.125 \alpha_{2}^s r}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  + \frac{0.076954635474829 \left(\alpha_{0}^{c_s}\right)^{3} r}{c_{s}} + \frac{0.065244100954277 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{3} r}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  + \frac{0.36074999115751 \alpha_{2}^{c_s} r}{c_{s}} + \frac{0.036259602621199 \alpha_{0}^{c_s} r^{3}}{c_{s}^{3}} \\
&{}  + \frac{0.024554893470954 \alpha_{0}^s r^{3}}{c_{s}^{3}} + \frac{0.10320649151137 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{2} r^{2}}{c_{s}^{2}} \\
&{}  + \frac{0.21414669687749 \left(\alpha_{0}^{c_s}\right)^{2} r^{2}}{c_{s}^{2}} - \frac{0.11879209843874 \alpha_{1}^{c_s} r^{2}}{c_{s}^{2}} \\
&{}  - \frac{0.060109233906372 \alpha_{1}^s r^{2}}{c_{s}^{2}} \\

&{}  - \frac{0.0078125 \alpha_{1}^s r^{2} \log{\left(c_{s} \right)}^{2}}{c_{s}^{2}} \\

\subsection{$n=1$}
For $p=4$, the absolute cutoff is $W=6$:
\[\alpha_{1}^t=\sum_{d=2}^{6}R_{1,d}+O(\mathrm{SR}^{7}).\]
\paragraph{Absolute order 2.}
\begin{align*}
R_{1,2} &= \frac{0.015625 r^{2}}{c_{s}^{2}} + \frac{0.125 \alpha_{0}^{c_s} r}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  + \frac{0.125 \alpha_{0}^s r}{c_{s}}
\end{align*}

\paragraph{Absolute order 3.}
\begin{align*}
R_{1,3} &=  - \frac{0.0029296875 r^{3}}{c_{s}^{3}} + \frac{0.125 \alpha_{1}^s r}{c_{s}} \\

&{}  + \frac{0.125 \alpha_{0}^{c_s} \alpha_{0}^s r \log{\left(c_{s} \right)}}{c_{s}}
\end{align*}

\subsection{$n=2$}
For $p=4$, the absolute cutoff is $W=7$:
\[\alpha_{2}^t=\sum_{d=3}^{7}R_{2,d}+O(\mathrm{SR}^{8}).\]
\paragraph{Absolute order 3.}
\begin{align*}
R_{2,3} &=  - \frac{0.00390625 r^{3}}{c_{s}^{3}} + \frac{0.125 \alpha_{1}^{c_s} r}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  + \frac{0.125 \alpha_{1}^s r}{c_{s}} - \frac{0.125 \left(\alpha_{0}^{c_s}\right)^{2} r}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  - \frac{0.125 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{2} r}{c_{s}} - \frac{0.046875 \alpha_{0}^{c_s} r^{2}}{c_{s}^{2}} \\
&{}  - \frac{0.046875 \alpha_{0}^s r^{2}}{c_{s}^{2}} - \frac{0.25 \alpha_{0}^{c_s} \alpha_{0}^s r}{c_{s}}
\end{align*}

\paragraph{Absolute order 4.}
\begin{align*}
R_{2,4} &= \frac{0.0013427734375 r^{4}}{c_{s}^{4}} + \frac{0.125 \alpha_{2}^s r}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  + \frac{0.125 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{3} r}{c_{s}} + \frac{0.125 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{2} r^{2}}{c_{s}^{2}} \\
&{}  + \frac{0.025390625 \alpha_{0}^s r^{3}}{c_{s}^{3}} + \frac{0.28379535568268 \alpha_{2}^{c_s} r}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  + \frac{0.28379535568268 \left(\alpha_{0}^{c_s}\right)^{3} r}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  + \frac{0.24409651676201 \left(\alpha_{0}^{c_s}\right)^{2} r^{2}}{c_{s}^{2}} + \frac{0.040277689595252 \alpha_{0}^{c_s} r^{3}}{c_{s}^{3}} \\
&{}  - \frac{0.0625 \alpha_{1}^s r^{2}}{c_{s}^{2}} - \frac{0.12204825838101 \alpha_{1}^{c_s} r^{2}}{c_{s}^{2}} \\
&{}  + \frac{0.046875 \alpha_{1}^s r^{2} \log{\left(c_{s} \right)}}{c_{s}^{2}} \\
&{}  + \frac{0.94259071136537 \left(\alpha_{0}^{c_s}\right)^{2} \alpha_{0}^s r}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  + \frac{0.78379535568268 \alpha_{0}^{c_s} \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{2} r}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  + \frac{0.41597151676201 \alpha_{0}^{c_s} \alpha_{0}^s r^{2}}{c_{s}^{2}} - \frac{0.125 \alpha_{2}^s r \log{\left(c_{s} \right)}}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  - \frac{0.125 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{3} r \log{\left(c_{s} \right)}}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  - \frac{0.01171875 \alpha_{0}^s r^{3} \log{\left(c_{s} \right)}}{c_{s}^{3}} \\
&{}  - \frac{0.09375 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{2} r^{2} \log{\left(c_{s} \right)}}{c_{s}^{2}} - \frac{0.375 \alpha_{0}^s \alpha_{1}^s r}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  - \frac{0.81759071136537 \alpha_{0}^s \alpha_{1}^{c_s} r}{c_{s}} - \frac{0.78379535568268 \alpha_{0}^{c_s} \alpha_{1}^s r}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  - \frac{0.85138606704805 \alpha_{0}^{c_s} \alpha_{1}^{c_s} r}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  + \frac{0.125 \alpha_{0}^s \alpha_{1}^{c_s} r \log{\left(c_{s} \right)}}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  + \frac{0.25 \alpha_{0}^{c_s} \alpha_{1}^s r \log{\left(c_{s} \right)}}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  + \frac{0.375 \alpha_{0}^s \alpha_{1}^s r \log{\left(c_{s} \right)}}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  - \frac{0.125 \left(\alpha_{0}^{c_s}\right)^{2} \alpha_{0}^s r \log{\left(c_{s} \right)}}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  - \frac{0.25 \alpha_{0}^{c_s} \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{2} r \log{\left(c_{s} \right)}}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  - \frac{0.09375 \alpha_{0}^{c_s} \alpha_{0}^s r^{2} \log{\left(c_{s} \right)}}{c_{s}^{2}}
\end{align*}

\paragraph{Absolute order 5.}
\begin{align*}
R_{2,5} &=  - \frac{0.00099348164677838 r^{5}}{c_{s}^{5}} + \frac{0.125 \alpha_{3}^s r}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  + \frac{0.050668108270534 \alpha_{1}^s r^{3}}{c_{s}^{3}} + \frac{0.33325502603924 \alpha_{3}^{c_s} r}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  + \frac{0.087560258989506 \alpha_{1}^{c_s} r^{3}}{c_{s}^{3}} - \frac{0.24566880066715 \left(\alpha_{0}^{c_s}\right)^{2} r^{3}}{c_{s}^{3}} \\

\subsection{$n=3$}
For $p=4$, the absolute cutoff is $W=8$:
\[\alpha_{3}^t=\sum_{d=4}^{8}R_{3,d}+O(\mathrm{SR}^{9}).\]
\paragraph{Absolute order 4.}
\begin{align*}
R_{3,4} &= \frac{0.00146484375 r^{4}}{c_{s}^{4}} + \frac{0.125 \alpha_{2}^{c_s} r}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  + \frac{0.125 \alpha_{2}^s r}{c_{s}} + \frac{0.125 \left(\alpha_{0}^{c_s}\right)^{3} r}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  + \frac{0.125 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{3} r}{c_{s}} + \frac{0.0234375 \alpha_{0}^{c_s} r^{3}}{c_{s}^{3}} \\
&{}  + \frac{0.0234375 \alpha_{0}^s r^{3}}{c_{s}^{3}} + \frac{0.109375 \left(\alpha_{0}^{c_s}\right)^{2} r^{2}}{c_{s}^{2}} \\
&{}  + \frac{0.109375 \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{2} r^{2}}{c_{s}^{2}} - \frac{0.0625 \alpha_{1}^{c_s} r^{2}}{c_{s}^{2}} \\
&{}  - \frac{0.0625 \alpha_{1}^s r^{2}}{c_{s}^{2}} + \frac{0.375 \alpha_{0}^{c_s} \left(\alpha_{0}^s\right)^{2} r}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  + \frac{0.375 \left(\alpha_{0}^{c_s}\right)^{2} \alpha_{0}^s r}{c_{s}} + \frac{0.21875 \alpha_{0}^{c_s} \alpha_{0}^s r^{2}}{c_{s}^{2}} \\
&{}  - \frac{0.375 \alpha_{0}^{c_s} \alpha_{1}^{c_s} r}{c_{s}} - \frac{0.375 \alpha_{0}^{c_s} \alpha_{1}^s r}{c_{s}} \\
&{}  - \frac{0.375 \alpha_{0}^s \alpha_{1}^{c_s} r}{c_{s}} - \frac{0.375 \alpha_{0}^s \alpha_{1}^s r}{c_{s}}
\end{align*}
